    الإحصائيات

    تعداد StatsType (منذ: 7.1)
    تعداد لأنواع الإحصائيات
    القيم
    • cumulative -- الإحصائية تراكمية؛ القيمة يمكن أن تزيد فقط.
    • instant -- الإحصائية لحظية؛ القيمة يمكن أن تزيد أو تنقص.
    • peak -- الإحصائية هي القيمة القصوى؛ القيمة يمكن أن تزيد فقط.
    • linear-histogram -- الإحصائية هي رسم بياني خطي.
    • log2-histogram -- الإحصائية هي رسم بياني لوغاريتمي، مع دلو لكل قوة من اثنين.
    تعداد StatsUnit (منذ: 7.1)
    تعداد لوحدات القياس للإحصائيات
    القيم
    • bytes -- الإحصائية مبلغ عنها بالبايت.
    • seconds -- الإحصائية مبلغ عنها بالثواني.
    • cycles -- الإحصائية مبلغ عنها بدورات الساعة.
    • boolean -- الإحصائية هي قيمة منطقية.
    تعداد StatsProvider (منذ: 7.1)
    تعداد لمزودي الإحصائيات.
    القيم
    • kvm -- منذ 7.1
    • cryptodev -- منذ 8.0
    تعداد StatsTarget (منذ: 7.1)
    أنواع الكائنات التي يمكن طلب الإحصائيات عليها.
    القيم
    • vm -- الإحصائيات التي تنطبق على الجهاز الافتراضي بأكمله أو عملية QEMU بأكملها.
    • vcpu -- الإحصائيات التي تنطبق على وحدة معالجة افتراضية واحدة.
    • cryptodev -- الإحصائيات التي تنطبق على جهاز تشفير (منذ 8.0)
    كائن StatsRequest (منذ: 7.1)
    يشير إلى مجموعة من الإحصائيات التي يجب أن يتم إرجاعها بواسطة query-stats.
    الأعضاء
    • provider (StatsProvider) -- المزود الذي يتم إرجاع الإحصائيات له.
    • names ([string], اختياري) -- الإحصائيات التي سيتم إرجاعها (الكل إذا تم حذفه).
    كائن StatsVCPUFilter (منذ: 7.1)
    الأعضاء
    •
    vcpus ([string], اختياري) -- قائمة بمسارات QOM لكائنات vCPU المطلوبة.
    كائن StatsFilter (منذ: 7.1)
    وسائط أمر query-stats؛ تحدد هدفًا يتم طلب الإحصائيات له، وبشكل اختياري المجموعة الفرعية المطلوبة من المعلومات لهذا الهدف.
    الأعضاء
    • target (StatsTarget) -- نوع الكائنات المراد الاستعلام عنها. لاحظ أن كل هدف ممكن قد يتيح خيارات تصفية إضافية
    • providers ([StatsRequest], اختياري) -- المزودون الذين سيتم طلب الإحصائيات منهم، وبشكل اختياري القيم المسماة التي سيتم إرجاعها داخل كل مزود
    • عندما يكون target هو vcpu: أعضاء StatsVCPUFilter.

    Command\n query-stats (Since: 7.1)
    \n
    Return runtime-collected statistics for objects such as the VM or its\n vCPUs.\n

    The arguments are a StatsFilter and specify the\n provider and objects to return statistics about.

    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n

    \n

    \n
    The members of StatsFilter.
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Return
    \n
    [StatsResult] -- a list of statistics, one for each\n provider and object (e.g., for each vCPU).

    Object\n StatsSchemaValue (Since: 7.1)
    \n
    Schema for a single statistic.
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Members
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
      \n
    • name (string) -- name of the statistic; each element of the\n schema is uniquely identified by a target, a provider (both available in\n StatsSchema) and the name.
      • \n
    • type (StatsType) -- kind of statistic.
      • \n
    • unit (StatsUnit, optional) -- basic unit of measure\n for the statistic; if missing, the statistic is a simple number or\n counter.
      • \n
    • base (int, optional) -- base for the multiple of\n unit in which the statistic is measured. Only present if\n exponent is non-zero; base and exponent together form\n a SI prefix (e.g., _nano-_ for base=10 and exponent=-9) or\n IEC binary prefix (e.g. _kibi-_ for base=2 and\n exponent=10)
      • \n
    • exponent (int) -- exponent for the multiple of unit\n in which the statistic is expressed, or 0 for the basic unit
      • \n
    • bucket-size (int, optional) -- Present when\n type is "linear-histogram", contains the width of each\n bucket of the histogram.
      • \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n

    \n
    \n
    Runtime-collected statistics and their names fall outside\n QEMU's usual deprecation policies. QEMU will try to keep the set of available\n data stable, together with their names, but will not guarantee stability at\n all costs; the same is true of providers that source statistics externally, e.g. from Linux. For example, if the same value is being tracked with\n different names on different architectures or by different providers, one of\n them might be renamed. A statistic might go away if an algorithm is changed or\n some code is removed; changing a default might cause previously useful\n statistics to always report 0. Such changes, however, are expected to be\n rare.
    \n
    \n
    \n\n
    \n

    VIRTIO DEVICES

    \n
    \n
    \n
    Object\n VirtioInfo (Since: 7.2)
    \n
    Basic information about a given VirtIODevice
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Members
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
      \n
    • path (string) -- The VirtIODevice's canonical QOM path
      • \n
    • name (string) -- Name of the VirtIODevice
      • \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Command\n x-query-virtio (Since: 7.2)
    \n
    This command is unstable/experimental.\n

    Return a list of all realized VirtIODevices

    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Features
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    •
    \n
    unstable -- This command is meant for debugging.
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Return
    \n
    [VirtioInfo] -- List of gathered VirtIODevices
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Example:
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    -> { "execute": "x-query-virtio" }\n<- { "return": [\n         {\n             "name": "virtio-input",\n             "path": "/machine/peripheral-anon/device[4]/virtio-backend"\n         },\n         {\n             "name": "virtio-crypto",\n             "path": "/machine/peripheral/crypto0/virtio-backend"\n         },\n         {\n             "name": "virtio-scsi",\n             "path": "/machine/peripheral-anon/device[2]/virtio-backend"\n         },\n         {\n             "name": "virtio-net",\n             "path": "/machine/peripheral-anon/device[1]/virtio-backend"\n         },\n         {\n             "name": "virtio-serial",\n             "path": "/machine/peripheral-anon/device[0]/virtio-backend"\n         }\n     ]\n   }
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Object\n VhostStatus (Since: 7.2)
    \n
    Information about a vhost device. This information will only be displayed\n if the vhost device is active.
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Members
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
      \n
    • n-mem-sections (int) -- vhost_dev n_mem_sections
      • \n
    • n-tmp-sections (int) -- vhost_dev n_tmp_sections
      • \n
    • nvqs (int) -- vhost_dev nvqs (number of virtqueues being\n used)
      • \n
    • vq-index (int) -- vhost_dev vq_index
      • \n
    • features (VirtioDeviceFeatures) -- vhost_dev features
      • \n
    • acked-features (VirtioDeviceFeatures) -- vhost_dev\n acked_features
      • \n
    • backend-features (VirtioDeviceFeatures) -- vhost_dev\n backend_features
      • \n
    • protocol-features (VhostDeviceProtocols) -- vhost_dev\n protocol_features
      • \n
    • max-queues (int) -- vhost_dev max_queues
      • \n
    • backend-cap (int) -- vhost_dev backend_cap
      • \n
    • log-enabled (boolean) -- vhost_dev log_enabled flag
      • \n
    • log-size (int) -- vhost_dev log_size
      • \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Object\n VirtioStatus (Since: 7.2)
    \n
    Full status of the virtio device with most VirtIODevice members. Also\n includes the full status of the corresponding vhost device if the vhost\n device is active.
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
    Members
    \n
    \n
    \n
    \n
      \n
    • name (string) -- VirtIODevice name
      • \n
    • device-id (int) -- VirtIODevice ID
      • \n
    • vhost-started (boolean) -- VirtIODevice vhost_started\n flag
      • \n
    • guest-features (VirtioDeviceFeatures) -- VirtIODevice\n guest_features
      • \n
    • host-features (VirtioDeviceFeatures) -- VirtIODevice\n host_features
      • \n
    • backend-features (VirtioDeviceFeatures) -- VirtIODevice\n backend_features
      • \n
    • device-endian (string) -- VirtIODevice device_endian
      • \n
    • num-vqs (int) -- VirtIODevice virtqueue count. This is the\n number of active virtqueues being used by the VirtIODevice.
      • \n
    • status (VirtioDeviceStatus) -- VirtIODevice configuration\n status (VirtioDeviceStatus)
      • \n
    • isr" }
      • device-endian (string) -- VirtIODevice device_endian
      • name (string) -- VirtIODevice name
      • device-id (int) -- VirtIODevice device_id
      • num-vqs (int) -- VirtIODevice num_vqs
      • status (VirtioConfigStatus) -- VirtIODevice status field
      • guest-features (VirtioFeatures) -- VirtIODevice guest_features
      • host-features (VirtioFeatures) -- VirtIODevice host_features
      • backend-features (VirtioFeatures) -- VirtIODevice backend_features
      • queue-num (int) -- VirtIODevice queue_num
      • isr (int) -- VirtIODevice ISR
      • queue-sel (int) -- VirtIODevice queue_sel
      • vm-running (boolean) -- VirtIODevice vm_running flag
      • broken (boolean) -- VirtIODevice broken flag
      • disabled (boolean) -- VirtIODevice disabled flag
      • use-started (boolean) -- VirtIODevice use_started flag
      • started (boolean) -- VirtIODevice started flag
      • start-on-kick (boolean) -- VirtIODevice start_on_kick flag
      • disable-legacy-check (boolean) -- VirtIODevice disabled_legacy_check flag
      • bus-name (string) -- VirtIODevice bus_name
      • use-guest-notifier-mask (boolean) -- VirtIODevice use_guest_notifier_mask flag
      • vhost-dev (VhostStatus, optional) -- Corresponding vhost device info for a given VirtIODevice. Present if the given VirtIODevice has an active vhost device.
      ```
    الأمر x-query-virtio-status (منذ: 7.2)
    هذا الأمر غير مستقر/تجريبي.

    استطلاع حالة شاملة لجهاز virtio معين

    الوسائط
    •
    path (string) -- المسار القياسي QOM لجهاز VirtIODevice
    الميزات
    •
    unstable -- هذا الأمر مخصص للتصحيح.
    العودة
    VirtioStatus -- حالة جهاز virtio

```text
UEFI VARIABLE STORE
The QEMU efi variable store implementation (hw/uefi/) uses this to store non-volatile variables in json format on disk.

This is an existing format already supported by (at least) two other projects, specifically https://gitlab.com/kraxel/virt-firmware and https://github.com/awslabs/python-uefivars.

Object UefiVariable (Since: 10.0)
UEFI Variable. Check the UEFI specifification for more detailed information on the fields.

Members
guid (string) -- variable namespace GUID
name (string) -- variable name, in UTF-8 encoding.
attr (int) -- variable attributes.
data (string) -- variable value, encoded as hex string.
time (string, optional) -- variable modification time. EFI_TIME struct, encoded as hex string. Used only for authenticated variables, where the EFI_VARIABLE_TIME_BASED_AUTHENTICATED_WRITE_ACCESS attribute bit is set.
digest (string, optional) -- variable certificate digest. Used to verify the signature of updates for authenticated variables. UEFI has two kinds of authenticated variables. The secure boot variables ('PK', 'KEK', 'db' and 'dbx') have hard coded signature checking rules. For other authenticated variables the firmware stores a digest of the signing certificate at variable creation time, and any updates must be signed with the same certificate.

    

    هذا الموقع غير مرتبط بـ Arch Linux ولا تابع له بأي شكل من الأشكال. المحتوى هو ترجمة فقط لصفحات دليل Arch المنشورة على man.archlinux.org.
